08/29/2023 00:00:00|"Nontechnical description"|"GENSOIL"|"generated"|"The Georgia component makes up 85 percent of the map unit. Slopes are 0 to 15 percent. This component is on hills on glaciated uplands, knolls on glaciated uplands. The parent material consists of coarse-loamy till. Depth to a root restrictive layer is greater than 60 inches. This component is on terraces on lake plains. The parent material consists of sandy glaciofluvial deposits over loamy glaciolacustrine deposits. Depth to a root restrictive layer is greater than 60 inches. The natural drainage class is well drained.  Water movement in the most restrictive layer is moderately high.  Available water to a depth of 60 inches (or restricted depth) is moderate.  Shrink-swell potential is low. This soil is not flooded. It is not ponded. A seasonal zone of water saturation is at 36 inches during March, April, May. Organic matter content in the surface horizon is about 5 percent. This component is on drumlinoid ridges, till plains. The parent material consists of loamy lodgement till derived from limestone. Depth to a root restrictive layer is greater than 60 inches. This component is on terraces on glacial lake plains. The parent material consists of clayey glaciolacustrine deposits. Depth to a root restrictive layer is greater than 60 inches. The natural drainage class is moderately well drained.  Water movement in the most restrictive layer is very low.  Available water to a depth of 60 inches (or restricted depth) is moderate.  Shrink-swell potential is moderate. This soil is not flooded. It is not ponded. A seasonal zone of water saturation is at 24 inches during January, February, March, April, May, December. Organic matter content in the surface horizon is about 4 percent. This component is in the F142XB005VT Clayplain ecological site. Nonirrigated land capability classification is 3e.  This soil does not meet hydric criteria.   "|"27657592"|"30711131"
